But in the touch handlers of the `GestureDetector` that action was gated behind the unrelated *"Show history on touch"* preference (`history-onclick`):\n\n```java\nif (!prefs.getBoolean("double-tap", false)) {\n    if (prefs.getBoolean("history-onclick", false)) {   // <-- gate\n        doAction("display-history");\n    }\n}\n```\n\nSo if the user doesn\'t also enable "Show history on touch" (which the repro steps never do, and which is a *history* setting, not a favorites one), tapping the screen never runs the action and the favorites bar can never be revealed — exactly the reported behaviour ("no favorites bar, even after tapping the screen"). This also contradicts `qa-suite.md`, which states that with minimalistic mode for favorites, "Touching the screen on an empty area displays history and the favorites".\n\n## Fix
